## What UK customers say

### Complaints

| Theme | Share | Reviews | Quote | Fix |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Thin / tinny metal | 5% | 4 | “Very tinny, but looks OK with a candle in it” | Specify a heavier metal gauge and state it on the spec sheet. |
| Loose glass, bent door or catches | 5% | 4 | “all glass was loose when we opened it” | Secure glass with clips and silicone; QC-test doors and latches before packing. |
| Glass broken or damaged in transit | 4% | 3 | “the large one arrived with a significant smash” | Use double-wall cartons with moulded pulp inserts, mark FRAGILE; drop-test to ISTA 1A. |
| Poor finish: paint chips, sharp edges | 4% | 3 | “a few paint chips around the neck” | Deburr all edges and add paint-finish QC; use durable baked coating. |
| Nested set hard to unpack | 4% | 3 | “Slightly tricky to get smaller lantern out of the large one” | Pack nested lanterns separately or add a pull-tab insert for easy removal. |

## Pitches

### Online Lighting

**Subject:** Brass hurricane lanterns to sit alongside your lantern pendants

Hello Online Lighting team,

I came across your range of lantern pendants, including the Searchlight 576RU Oil Lantern Pendant, and thought our work might suit your customers.

We make brass hurricane lanterns in an antique gold finish in Moradabad, India. Under the India-UK CETA, these goods enter the UK at zero import duty, with proof of origin supplied. Our minimum order is 200 pieces, and our quote range is £7.73–£13.92 FOB per piece.

We've worked on the details buyers care about. We use a heavier metal gauge, stated on the spec sheet. Each lantern ships in a double-wall carton with moulded pulp inserts, drop-tested to ISTA 1A, so the glass arrives intact.

Would you like me to send a catalogue and arrange samples?

Kind regards,
[Your name], [Company], Moradabad

### Electricpoint

**Subject:** Antique brass lanterns at trade terms, zero UK duty

Hello Electricpoint team,

I noticed you list the Searchlight Antique Brass Glass Domed lantern alongside your trade-priced range, so I wanted to introduce a direct-from-maker option.

We manufacture brass hurricane lanterns in an antique gold finish in Moradabad, India. Under the India-UK CETA they enter the UK at zero import duty, with proof of origin provided. Our MOQ is 200 pieces, and our quote range is £7.73–£13.92 FOB per piece.

For trade buyers we focus on fewer returns. Glass is secured with clips and silicone, and every door and latch is QC-tested before packing. Cartons are double-wall with moulded pulp inserts and are drop-tested to ISTA 1A.

## Spec improvements

1. Use a heavier brass gauge and state the thickness on the spec sheet so the lantern does not feel tinny.
2. Hold every glass panel with metal clips and silicone, and check each door and latch before packing.
3. Pack each lantern in a double-wall carton with moulded pulp inserts, mark it FRAGILE, and drop-test to ISTA 1A.
4. Deburr all edges and use a durable baked coating, with a finish check to catch paint chips.
5. For nested sets, pack each lantern separately or add a pull-tab insert so the smaller one lifts out easily.

## Compliance notes

- UK: products must carry a UK-responsible person/importer address on packaging (GPSR equivalent under UK product safety rules).
- Candle holders and lanterns: include fire-safety warnings for use with candles.
- Food-contact items (bowls, trays, barware): brass must be lined/lacquered and meet UK food-contact rules.
- CETA zero duty needs a valid proof of origin (statement on origin / certificate) — verify before quoting.

## Assumptions

| Assumption | Value |
|---|---|
| UK VAT | 20% |
| Retailer markup | 2.2× |
| Importer / wholesaler markup | 1.8× |
| Freight, insurance and clearance (share of FOB) | 15% |
| Import duty from India | 0% |
| Exchange rate (GBP→INR, live) | 127.44 |
